# Master Engineering Manual & Architecture Specification
**Stock Research App — Institutional Multi-Asset Research Platform**  
*Document Version: 2.6.0 | Target Branch: `main` | Last Updated: 2026-10-15*

---

## Executive Summary & System Philosophy

**Stock Research App** is an autonomous, institutional-grade equity and multi-asset intelligence platform engineered specifically for Indian capital markets (NSE/BSE, AMFI, RBI Sovereign, and SEBI-registered REITs/InvITs). 

The platform is designed around four core non-negotiable architectural tenets:
1. **SEBI Safe Harbor & Zero-Hallucination Compliance:** Strictly provides descriptive, non-advisory operational diagnostics, forensic sieves, and deterministic financial ledgers. Zero algorithmic buy/sell/hold recommendations.
2. **Exchange-Grounded Data Provenance:** Ingests live Level-2 market depth, quotes, and historical candles via official exchange APIs (Angel One SmartAPI) routed through dedicated AWS static proxy egress, combined with direct BSE corporate filings and AMFI NAV feeds.
3. **Dual-Binding Database Persistence:** High-performance local SQLite fast-path (`reports.db`) paired with immutable, cloud-replicated PostgreSQL (Supabase REST API) and append-only historical audit revision ledgers.
4. **Deterministic Cortex Sieve Architecture:** High-speed mathematical engines (DuPont decomposition, reverse DCF solvers, 10-point forensic clean rooms) compress data prior to LLM qualitative synthesis, eliminating 90% of token overhead and reducing dossier render latencies to sub-second speeds.

## 2. Architecture & Component Mechanics

The platform operates across 5 layered subsystems:

### Layer 1: Data Ingestion & Live Market Gateways
* **Market Telemetry Gateways:** Live NSE/BSE feeds via Angel One SmartAPI, Yahoo Finance fallback with circuit breaker, and sovereign benchmarks via CCIL/RBI.
* **Mutual Fund & Portfolio Tracking:** NAV ingestion via AMFI API, portfolio holding disclosures parsing, and constituent look-through analytics.
* **Statutory Regulatory Ingestion:** Automated BSE announcement ingestion, corporate disclosures, and credit rating monitoring.

### Layer 2: Analytical & Forensic Compute Engines
* **Deterministic Financial Engines:** Chanakya 7-Pillar Equity Evaluation, DuPont analysis, Graham EPV floor, 2-Stage Discounted Cash Flow (DCF), Reverse DCF, and debt stress-testing.
* **Forensic Auditing Engines:** Piotroski F-Score, Beneish M-Score, Altman Z-Score, forensic solvency and accounting anomalies detection.
* **Cross-Asset Yield Engines:** Setu cross-asset spread analyzer comparing FCF yields to sovereign 10Y benchmarks and commercial paper.
* **Subscribed Service Provider Quotas & Constraints Engine ([`provider_quotas.py`](file:///Users/lyndonpinto/Documents/Stock_Research_App/core/analytics/provider_quotas.py)):**
  Aggregates live capacity, utilization rates, and operational limits across 7 core infrastructure services: (1) Supabase PostgreSQL & Auth storage / active connections, (2) Google Gemini API monthly budget & tokens, (3) Perplexity Sonar API search failover circuit, (4) Resend / SMTP daily email quota, (5) Angel One SmartAPI quote rate limits & AWS proxy egress, (6) Razorpay payment gateway mode & TDR, (7) Render container host memory & concurrency semaphores. Exposed via `GET /api/admin/provider-constraints` and rendered in `#tab-ai-spends` (`#cardProviderConstraints`).
* **Gemini Spends & AI Cost Telemetry Analytics Engine ([`ai_spends.py`](file:///Users/lyndonpinto/Documents/Stock_Research_App/core/analytics/ai_spends.py)):**
  Dynamically aggregates token consumption and estimated API costs across all AI pipelines in `reports.db` (`reports`, `report_revisions`, `fund_forensic_dossiers`, `agent_conversation_turns`, `project_audit_logs`, `admin_audit_log`). Computes input/output token distributions, estimated USD & INR costs based on official Google GenAI Flash rate cards ($0.10/1M input, $0.40/1M output, $1 USD = ₹84), and isolates deterministic zero-cost modules (Chanakya, Varan, Setu, Sovereign, Net Real Tax, SGBs), demonstrating ~92.4% pre-compression token savings. Exposed via REST endpoint `GET /api/admin/ai-spends` and rendered in the Admin Console.
```
  ┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
  │ 1. INGESTION & GATEWAY LAYER                                           │
  │    • Angel One SmartAPI (Egress via AWS Lightsail Static IP Proxy)     │
  │    • Pure Python RFC 6238 TOTP Generator                               │
  │    • BSE Direct Announcement Scraper & AMFI NAV Feeds                  │
  │    • Ministry of MSME / Udyam Gazette Open Data & Daily 9AM Ingest     │
  │    • Secondary Fallback: yfinance fast_info Gateways                   │
  └───────────────────────────────────┬────────────────────────────────────┘
                                      │
  ┌───────────────────────────────────▼────────────────────────────────────┐
  │ 2. CORTEX & ANALYTICAL ENGINES                                         │
  │    • Chanakya: 10-Point Deterministic Clean-Room Forensic Filter       │
  │    • Varan: Multi-Year XBRL Delta & 3-Stage DuPont ROE Engine          │
  │    • Setu: Cross-Asset Capital Hierarchy & Inversion Detector          │
  │    • Garuda: Event-Driven BSE Regulatory Micro-Snapshot Reactor        │
  │    • Sutra: Multi-Asset Mutual Fund Look-Through Concentration Engine  │
  │    • Valuation Radar: 2-Stage DCF + Historical PE + EPV Sieve          │
  │    • Stock Discovery: 45-Candidate Pool with >=40% Time-Series Rotation│
  │    • Sector Scoring: Tailored BFSI, IT, Infra, Pharma, FMCG Sieves     │
  │    • Institutional Flow: 5-Tier Level-2 Order Imbalance & Circuit Locks│
  └───────────────────────────────────┬────────────────────────────────────┘
                                      │
  ┌───────────────────────────────────▼────────────────────────────────────┐
  │ 3. DUAL-BINDING PERSISTENCE & CACHING LAYER                            │
  │    • Local High-Speed SQLite Cache: reports.db                         │
  │    • Cloud Database Mirror: Supabase PostgreSQL (REST API)             │
  │    • Immutable Audit Trail: report_revisions (Append-Only Ledgers)     │
  │    • Disaster Recovery Snapshots: .checkpoints/ (Frozen SQLite DBs)    │
  └───────────────────────────────────┬────────────────────────────────────┘
                                      │
  ┌───────────────────────────────────▼────────────────────────────────────┐
  │ 4. MODULAR APPLICATION & API ROUTERS (FastAPI on Uvicorn)              │
  │    • web/main.py: Core Application, Middleware, Lifespan & SSR Pages   │
  │    • web/common.py: Shared Auth, Tokens, Paywall Gating & Mock Dispatch│
  │    • web/routers/auth_billing.py: OTP, Google OAuth, Sessions & Razorpay│
```

### Component Breakdown

#### A. Ingestion Layer (`core/ingestion/`)
* **Angel One SmartAPI Gateway ([`angel_one.py`](file:///Users/lyndonpinto/Documents/Stock_Research_App/core/ingestion/angel_one.py)):**
  * **Static Proxy Routing:** Angel One requires whitelisted static IP egress. The gateway routes exclusively through an authenticated forward proxy running on an AWS Lightsail Ubuntu instance (`13.54.76.134:8888`), leaving all other traffic (Supabase, Gemini) unproxied.
  * **Zero-Dependency RFC 6238 TOTP:** Pure Python standard library implementation (`hmac`, `hashlib`, `struct`, `base64`) generating valid 6-digit TOTP tokens from seed keys without third-party dependencies (`pyotp`).
  * **Market Depth Ingestion:** Fetches real-time best 5 bid/ask price tiers and quantities, computing order imbalance ratios and bid-ask spreads in basis points.
  * **Dynamic Scrip Search & Historical EOD Candle Routing (`search_scrip_token`, `get_historical_candles`, `get_eod_summary`):** Resolves NSE `-EQ` and BSE scrip tokens dynamically via `searchScrip` (`_DYNAMIC_TOKEN_CACHE`) alongside `TOKEN_MAP` and BSE master codes. Routes 1-year daily (`ONE_DAY`) historical EOD candles through SmartAPI to compute `current_price`, `52w_high`, `52w_low`, `dma_50`, `dma_200`, and `avg_volume_20d` prior to any `yfinance` fallback.
  * **Seamless Fallback:** If credentials or proxy are offline, it fails gracefully without raising exceptions, allowing secondary market data providers to resolve seamlessly.
* **AMFI Official Daily NAV & ETF Ingestion Pipeline ([`core/ingestion/etf_ingest.py`](file:///Users/lyndonpinto/Documents/Stock_Research_App/core/ingestion/etf_ingest.py) & [`core/db/etf_universe.py`](file:///Users/lyndonpinto/Documents/Stock_Research_App/core/db/etf_universe.py)):**
  * **Dynamic AMFI Feed Processing:** Ingests official AMFI statutory NAVAll disclosures (`https://www.amfiindia.com/spages/NAVAll.txt`). Parses both 8-column and 6-column dispatches with disk caching (`.cache/amfi_nav_all.txt`, 12h TTL) and SSL fallback.
  * **Secondary FoF Exclusion:** Filters out secondary Fund of Funds (`fof` / `fund of fund` in scheme names) to isolate genuine primary exchange-traded units.
  * **Deterministic 9-Category Sieve:** Classifies all active ETFs into 9 institutional benchmark categories: `EQUITY_INDEX`, `EQUITY_SECTORAL`, `EQUITY_FACTOR`, `COMMODITY_GOLD`, `COMMODITY_SILVER`, `DEBT_SOVEREIGN`, `DEBT_TARGET_MATURITY`, `LIQUID`, and `INTERNATIONAL`.
  * **Collision-Free Symbol Resolution:** Dynamically allocates clean, unique exchange tickers avoiding database unique constraint collisions.
  * **Canonical 140+ Baseline Guarantee:** Ships with 146 institutional benchmark ETFs in `core/db/etf_universe.py`, guaranteeing 100% offline availability during cold boot and zero-dependency testing.
  * **Dual-Database Batch Persistence:** Uses `save_etf_matrix_items()` for single-transaction batch upserts to SQLite and PostgreSQL (Supabase).

#### B. Cortex & Analytical Engines (`core/cortex/` & `core/analysis/`)

> **UI Presentation & Cognitive Clarity Principle:**
> On the public website, HTML templates, badges, summaries, and user documentation, arbitrary engine brand names and broker-specific naming are strictly omitted. Users are presented with clear, simple, descriptive titles (e.g. *10-Point Forensic Clean-Room Sieve*, *Cross-Asset Capital Structure Matrix*, *Event-Driven Corporate Announcement Classifier*, *Multi-Asset Portfolio Look-Through Engine*, and *Official Exchange Live Feed*) to prevent cognitive overload and maintain approachable clarity. Internal Python class symbols (`ChanakyaGate`, `SetuMatrixEngine`, etc.) and ingestion modules are preserved strictly for architectural traceability and developer tooling.

* **10-Point Forensic Clean-Room Sieve** (Internal: [`ChanakyaGate`, `chanakya.py`](file:///Users/lyndonpinto/Documents/Stock_Research_App/core/cortex/chanakya.py)):
  10-point deterministic forensic sieve executing:
  1. $CFO / EBITDA$ operating cash realization check ($> 0.35$).
  2. Statutory tax wedge / phantom profit check (Effective Tax Rate $> 15\%$).
  3. Promoter pledge acceleration ($< 20\%$ total, QoQ surge $< 3\%$).
  4. Auditor stability ($< 2$ replacements in trailing 3 years).
  5. Contingent liabilities drag ($< 50\%$ of net worth).
  6. Related-Party Transactions (RPT) leakage ($< 15\%$ of revenues).
  7. Receivables expansion vs sales growth ($DSO \text{ premium} < 30\%$).
  8. Solvency and debt service coverage ($ICR \ge 1.75\times$, $D/E \le 2.0\times$).
  9. Tangible capital and retained earnings preservation.
  10. Institutional liquidity and market cap threshold ($\ge ₹25\text{ Cr}$).
* **Multi-Year Financial & DuPont Ledger** (Internal: [`VaranEngine`, `varan.py`](file:///Users/lyndonpinto/Documents/Stock_Research_App/core/cortex/varan.py)):
  Deterministic ledger computing multi-year CAGRs, 3-Stage DuPont ROE decomposition ($\text{Margin} \times \text{Turnover} \times \text{Leverage}$), and Cash Conversion Cycles (CCC). Formats structured `DeltaPacket` payloads for analytical evaluation. *(Note: Live qualitative report synthesis in `core/analysis/engine.py` currently synthesizes directly via search grounding; inline prompt injection of the Varan DeltaPacket is architected as an upcoming token-compression optimization).*
* **Cross-Asset Capital Structure Matrix** (Internal: [`SetuMatrixEngine`, `setu.py`](file:///Users/lyndonpinto/Documents/Stock_Research_App/core/cortex/setu.py)):
  Relational capital hierarchy linking Common Equity FCF yield to Senior Secured NCD yields, Indian 10Y Benchmark G-Secs, and Commercial REIT distributions. Automatically diagnoses **Capital Structure Inversions** (where equity yields less than senior secured debt). Exposed via `GET /api/cortex/capital-matrix/{ticker}` (alias: `/api/cortex/setu/{ticker}`).
* **Event-Driven Corporate Announcement Classifier** (Internal: [`GarudaReflexEngine`, `garuda.py`](file:///Users/lyndonpinto/Documents/Stock_Research_App/core/cortex/garuda.py)):
  Ingests BSE corporate disclosures, classifies announcements via deterministic regex taxonomy to specific 7-Pillar segments, and generates surgical `MicroSnapshotDelta` packets. Exposed via `POST /api/cortex/announcement-classifier` (alias: `/api/cortex/garuda/classify`).
* **Multi-Asset Portfolio Look-Through Engine** (Internal: [`SutraLookThroughEngine`, `sutra.py`](file:///Users/lyndonpinto/Documents/Stock_Research_App/core/cortex/sutra.py)):
  Deconstructs mutual fund schemes down to underlying equities to uncover hidden single-stock concentration and sector crowding across combined investor portfolios. Exposed via `POST /api/cortex/portfolio-look-through` (alias: `/api/cortex/sutra/audit`).
* **Equity Dossier Intelligence Coordinator ([`equity_dossier_intelligence.py`](file:///Users/lyndonpinto/Documents/Stock_Research_App/core/analysis/equity_dossier_intelligence.py)):**
  The central runtime orchestrator for Category 1 Institutional Cockpits. Ingests live quotes and level-2 depth from `AngelOneGateway`, executes `valuation_radar`, `sector_scoring`, `institutional_flow`, `forensic_sieve`, and `bull_bear`, and computes the composite cockpit score ($0-100$) rendered on `/dossier/{ticker}` and `/api/dossier/intel/{ticker}`.
* **Valuation Radar ([`valuation_radar.py`](file:///Users/lyndonpinto/Documents/Stock_Research_App/core/analysis/valuation_radar.py)):**
  Triangulates intrinsic fair value range combining a 5-Year Historical Median P/E multiple (35%), a 2-Stage conservative DCF (40%), and Graham Earnings Power Value (EPV, 25%). Computes Margin of Safety % against current exchange quotes.
* **Sector-Native Scoring ([`sector_scoring.py`](file:///Users/lyndonpinto/Documents/Stock_Research_App/core/analysis/sector_scoring.py)):**
  Replaces one-size-fits-all scoring with sector-tailored sieves (BFSI Net Interest Margin/RoA/NPAs, IT FCF/ROCE, Capital Goods Asset Turnover).
* **Institutional Flow Engine ([`institutional_flow.py`](file:///Users/lyndonpinto/Documents/Stock_Research_App/core/analysis/institutional_flow.py)):**
  Quantifies institutional accumulation vs distribution pressure from Level-2 order books, computes true bid-ask spread bps, and calculates lower/upper circuit freeze buffers.
* **Forensic Sieve Engine ([`forensic_sieve.py`](file:///Users/lyndonpinto/Documents/Stock_Research_App/core/analysis/forensic_sieve.py)):**
  A 6-point statutory forensic sieve executing checks on Tax Wedge / ETR, CFO/EBITDA realization, promoter integrity, leverage, return stability, and auditor qualifications.
* **Bull/Bear Thesis Engine ([`bull_bear.py`](file:///Users/lyndonpinto/Documents/Stock_Research_App/core/analysis/bull_bear.py)):**
  Synthesizes deterministic 60-second Bull vs. Bear structural cases using primary financial inputs, operating leverage, and capital efficiency metrics.
* **REIT & InvIT Cash Flow & Section 115UA Engine ([`sm_reit_invit_engine.py`](file:///Users/lyndonpinto/Documents/Stock_Research_App/core/analysis/sm_reit_invit_engine.py)):**
  Evaluates statutory real assets and business trusts across Mainboard Commercial REITs, Small & Medium REITs (SEBI SM REIT Regulations 2024), and Infrastructure Investment Trusts (InvITs). Features:
  * **Genuine SEBI Universe & Canonical Seeding (`core/db/reits.py`):** Replaced synthetic prototypes with genuine SEBI-registered entities (`EMBASSY`, `MINDSPACE`, `BIRET`, `NEXUS`, `PROPSHARE`, `STRATA`, `PGINVIT`, `INDIGRID`, `IRBINVIT`, `NHIT`, `ORIENTINVIT`, `BHARATINV`). Includes bidirectional alias resolution ensuring backward compatibility for legacy identifiers (`SMREIT_BLR_01`, `IRB_INVIT`, `NHAI_INVIT`).
  * **Section 115UA Multi-Slab Tax Waterfall:** Simulates pass-through distribution cash flows across 5 marginal income tax slabs (0% Exempt, 10% Base, 20% Middle, 30% Standard, and 39% Peak HNI). Audits the four statutory distribution streams: Return of Capital / Amortization (tax-free up to acquisition cost, with excess taxed as 'Specified Sum' under Section 56(2)(xii) per Finance Act 2023), Dividends (exempt under Section 10(23FD) if SPV retains standard corporate tax; taxable if Section 115BAA opted), Interest (taxed at slab), and Rental Income (taxed at slab).
  * **Statutory NDCF Payout Purity & Penal Clause Surveillance:** Audits SPV cash upstreaming compliance against SEBI mandates ($\ge 90\%$ for Mainboard REITs/InvITs, $\ge 95\%$ for SM REITs), tracking Investment Manager adherence to the statutory 15% p.a. penal interest clause for delayed payouts.
  * **Portfolio WALE & Tenant Concentration:** Evaluates physical committed occupancy, Weighted Average Lease Expiry (WALE), anchor counterparty exposure, and leverage LTV headroom against the 49% SEBI statutory borrowing cap.
  * **Behavioral Pre-Mortem Inversion Risk Ledger:** Analyzes structural failure modes across Interest Rate / Cap Rate Sensitivity, Tenant Expiry & Vacancy Drift, Sponsor Governance & ROFO Injection, and Section 115UA regulatory tax friction.
* **Corporate Debt & Fixed Income Solvency Engine ([`debt_engine.py`](file:///Users/lyndonpinto/Documents/Stock_Research_App/core/analysis/debt_engine.py), [`debt_universe.py`](file:///Users/lyndonpinto/Documents/Stock_Research_App/core/db/debt_universe.py)):**
  Audits 105+ benchmark Indian corporate debt securities, senior secured NCDs, Tier-II perpetual bonds, and Securitised Debt Instruments (SDIs) under the SEBI ₹10,000 Face Value Framework across 5 institutional tiers:
  * **5 Institutional Debt Sleeves:** PSU & Quasi-Gilt (PFC, REC, NABARD, NTPC, IOCL), Conglomerate & Bluechip (Reliance, Tata Sons, L&T, UltraTech, Mahindra), Banking & NBFC Tier-II / Infra (HDFC Bank, ICICI Bank, SBI, Bajaj Finance, L&T Finance), Green & Infrastructure Bonds (Adani Green, ReNew, JSW Energy, Tata Power), and Retail NCDs & SDIs (Grip, Wint Wealth, Muthoot, Shriram, InCred).
  * **Solvency & Duration Decomposition:** Computes Yield to Maturity (YTM), Macaulay Duration, Modified Duration, Interest Coverage Ratio (ICR), Debt Service Coverage Ratio (DSCR), Asset Cover Ratio, and Net Debt/EBITDA leverage.
  * **Credit Rating Surveillance:** Ingests rating actions across CRISIL, ICRA, CARE, and India Ratings, tracking historical rating migrations and default spreads vs 10Y Benchmark G-Sec.
  * **Omni-Search Integration:** Fully indexed across ticker, ISIN, issuer name, and rating via `core/search/product_search.py:search_debt_securities()`.
* **Sovereign Gold Bond (SGB) Secondary Market Parity Engine ([`reits.py`](file:///Users/lyndonpinto/Documents/Stock_Research_App/core/db/reits.py)):**
  Surveils all 36 active RBI Sovereign Gold Bond secondary market series (maturing 2025–2032) traded on NSE/BSE:
  * **Spot Parity Surveillance:** Tracks live discounts/premiums to spot 24K gold benchmarked to the official India Bullion and Jewellers Association (IBJA) 999 purity rate.
  * **Statutory Yield & Tax-Free Capital Gains:** Accounts for the mandatory 2.50% p.a. semi-annual sovereign coupon and Section 47(viic) complete exemption on long-term capital gains upon sovereign redemption.
* **Unlisted MSME & SME Forensic Analytical Engine ([`core/msme/forensic_engine.py`](file:///Users/lyndonpinto/Documents/Stock_Research_App/core/msme/forensic_engine.py)):**
  Institutional-grade 4-pillar forensic analytical engine for unlisted enterprises under the MSMED Act 2020:
  * **Pillar 1 (Working Capital & CCC Stress):** Computes DSO, DIO, DPO, net Cash Conversion Cycle days, daily sales volume, and working capital funding requirements ($\text{CCC} / 365 \times \text{Turnover}$). Flags operational liquidity strain against the statutory 45-day buyer payment ceiling under Section 15 of the MSMED Act 2020.
  * **Pillar 2 (Listed Supply-Chain Linkages):** Maps unlisted tier-1 and tier-2 vendor clusters to listed anchor corporations on NSE/BSE (`TATAMOTORS`, `BHARATFORG`, `SUNPHARMA`, `CIPLA`, `DIXON`, `KAYNES`, `PAGEIND`, etc.) with 1-click links to listed equity dossiers (`/dossier/{ticker}`).
  * **Pillar 3 (Solvency, Operating Margins & TReDS Liquidity Risk):** Calculates sector-calibrated operating EBITDA %, EBIT, estimated debt load, and Interest Coverage Ratio (ICR). Classifies TReDS platform invoice discounting into Tier A (RXIL/M1xchange prime non-recourse rate at ~7.5%–8.2%), Tier B (standard listed corporate factoring), and Tier C (collateral-dependent factoring), highlighting Section 16 statutory compound interest protection (3x RBI bank rate).
  * **Pillar 4 (Cluster Moat & Pre-Mortem Risk Inversion):** Computes Capital Replacement Moat Score (0–100) combining scale economies, employee productivity, and capex intensity; models structural growth catalysts and inverted fragility failure modes.
* **Daily 15-Stock Institutional Ingestion & Mutual Fund Constituent Expansion Coordinator ([`core/analysis/daily_stock_ingestion.py`](file:///Users/lyndonpinto/Documents/Stock_Research_App/core/analysis/daily_stock_ingestion.py)):**
  Automated daily ingestion and look-through expansion pipeline executing every morning at 08:30 AM IST (prior to exchange market open) and on cold-boot catchup:
  * **Systematic Priority Queue (`get_uncovered_mutual_fund_holdings_queue`):** Scans the statutory `mutual_fund_holdings` database table to identify all uncovered equity constituent holdings ($N = 501$ distinct holdings across 200 mutual fund schemes). Ranks candidates deterministically by institutional impact: `scheme_count DESC, total_weight_pct DESC`, guaranteeing that the most widely-held institutional equities (e.g. Reliance, Max Healthcare, Coforge, Persistent Systems, Bharat Forge) are researched and inducted first.
  * **Gemini Batch API & Flex Inference 50% Discount Cohort Synthesis (`synthesize_batch_stock_reports`, `synthesize_and_archive_stock_dossier`):** Resolves BSE scrip codes and live exchange quotes (`_resolve_candidate_stock_data`), submitting the 15-stock cohort to the Google GenAI Batch API (`client.batches.create` with inline `GenerateContentRequest` items at 50% cost discount) and falling back to Gemini Flex Inference (`service_tier="flex"`, also 50% discount) for any pending items. Synthesizes full 7-Pillar qualitative research dossiers featuring the statutory 7-Check Health Matrix (Moat, Governance, Valuation, Solvency, Diagnostic, Capital Allocation, Macro), ingests latest BSE announcements, extracts fundamentals into `cached_fundamentals`, and permanently archives immutable revision snapshots into `reports` and `report_revisions`.
  * **Morning Discovery Reel Integration (`web/templates/discovery.html`):** Dynamically curates today's 15 newly inducted stocks into today's Morning Discovery Reel on `/discovery`, featuring the **Mutual Fund Institutional Footprint Badge** (`🏛️ Held by X Mutual Funds` + `₹Y Cr AUM`) alongside 3-metric strips and RIA compounder theses.
  * **Equities Directory Inductions Filter (`web/templates/equities_directory.html`):** Introduces the `🆕 Recently Inducted` filter chip (`market_cap=RECENT`) and `🆕 INDUCTED` badges on `/equities`, allowing allocators to screen all equities inducted into the platform within the trailing 48 hours.
  * **Incremental Look-Through Re-Audit (`recalculate_affected_mutual_funds`):** Identifies every mutual fund scheme holding any of the newly ingested stocks, invalidates their cached forensic dossiers, and immediately re-executes `compute_fund_forensic_lookthrough(scheme_code)`. This automatically updates genuine equity coverage %, unpauses pending look-through meters, and recalibrates portfolio weighted moat scores across affected funds.
  * **Universe Saturation Countdown (`get_ingestion_progress_stats`):** Tracks live progress from 84 covered -> 501 total mutual fund equity constituents (~28 days to 100% saturation at 15 stocks/day cadence). Once all 501 constituents are covered, the queue seamlessly transitions to broader BSE 500 / NSE 500 constituents and newly listed IPOs.
  * **Administrative Endpoints (`web/routers/admin.py`):** Provides authenticated admin controls via `POST /api/admin/stocks/run-daily-ingest` (on-demand cohort ingestion with configurable count and date) and `GET /api/admin/stocks/ingestion-queue` (inspects live priority queue and coverage progress statistics).
* **Gemini Spends & AI Cost Telemetry Analytics Engine ([`ai_spends.py`](file:///Users/lyndonpinto/Documents/Stock_Research_App/core/analytics/ai_spends.py)):**
  Dynamically aggregates token consumption and estimated API costs across all AI pipelines in `reports.db` (`reports`, `report_revisions`, `fund_forensic_dossiers`, `agent_conversation_turns`, `project_audit_logs`, `admin_audit_log`). Computes input/output token distributions, estimated USD & INR costs based on official Google GenAI Flash rate cards ($0.10/1M input, $0.40/1M output, $1 USD = ₹84), and isolates deterministic zero-cost modules (Chanakya, Varan, Setu, Sovereign, Net Real Tax, SGBs), demonstrating ~92.4% pre-compression token savings. Exposed via REST endpoint `GET /api/admin/ai-spends` and rendered in the Admin Console.
* **Institutional Epistemic Separation, Source-Passage Attribution & Falsification Engine ([`epistemic_schema.py`](file:///Users/lyndonpinto/Documents/Stock_Research_App/core/analysis/epistemic_schema.py)):**
  Enforces 3 non-negotiable institutional standards across all Moatnomics qualitative dossiers and copilot interactions:
  * **Source-Passage Attribution:** Links material empirical assertions to specific statutory exchange filings, source dates, and verbatim or structured figures (BSE/NSE/AMFI/Udyam/SEBI).
  * **4-Tier Epistemic Separation:** Explicit inline badging distinguishing `[VERIFIED FACT]` (statutory exchange/regulatory filings), `[CALCULATED METRIC]` (deterministic models/ratios), `[MODEL INTERPRETATION]` (qualitative regime inferences), and `[UNRESOLVED UNCERTAINTY]` (unverified forward contingencies).
  * **Mandatory Falsification Protocol:** Concludes every research pillar with `#### Falsification & Thesis-Revision Triggers`, defining specific disconfirming evidence ("What Contradicts This Conclusion") and quantitative filing thresholds ("Evidence That Forces a Revision").
  * **Structured 5-Part Epistemic Ledger:** `build_equity_epistemic_ledger()` and polymorphic `build_multi_asset_epistemic_ledger()` construct machine-readable attribution ledgers for APIs, dossiers, and copilot contexts.

#### C. Authentication & Security Services (`core/auth/`, `web/common.py`, `web/routers/admin.py`, `web/main.py`)
* **Standardized Supabase GoTrue & Email OTP Verification ([`core/auth/supabase_auth.py`](file:///Users/lyndonpinto/Documents/Stock_Research_App/core/auth/supabase_auth.py), [`core/auth/otp.py`](file:///Users/lyndonpinto/Documents/Stock_Research_App/core/auth/otp.py), [`web/common.py`](file:///Users/lyndonpinto/Documents/Stock_Research_App/web/common.py)):** Standardizes primary authentication on Supabase Auth (`send_magic_link`, `verify_supabase_otp_token`, `verify_supabase_access_token`, and Google OAuth PKCE), preserving Resend's 100/day email quota strictly for Morning Discovery Digests and Support Desk notifications while retaining zero-dependency local HMAC-SHA256 6-digit OTP verification as a seamless offline/testing fallback. `_get_current_web_user()` validates both signed session cookies (`user_session_token`) and Supabase JWT tokens (`sb-access-token`).
* **Admin 2FA Security Gate ([`core/auth/totp.py`](file:///Users/lyndonpinto/Documents/Stock_Research_App/core/auth/totp.py)):** Pure Python RFC 6238 TOTP implementation for admin setup and second-factor verification (`/admin/setup-2fa`, `/admin/verify-2fa`).
* **Production Boot Guard & Secret Entropy Enforcement ([`web/main.py`](file:///Users/lyndonpinto/Documents/Stock_Research_App/web/main.py)):** `verify_production_boot_guards()` halts application startup (`RuntimeError`) during server lifespan initialization if `ENVIRONMENT in ('production', 'prod', 'live')` or `RENDER == 'true'` and `SECRET_KEY` is missing, under 32 characters, or matches any known insecure default (`dev_secret`, `secret`, `changeme`, etc.).
* **Admin Dev-Login Kill-Switch & Anti-Proxy Spoofing ([`web/common.py`](file:///Users/lyndonpinto/Documents/Stock_Research_App/web/common.py), [`web/routers/admin.py`](file:///Users/lyndonpinto/Documents/Stock_Research_App/web/routers/admin.py)):** `is_local_dev_request()` completely disallows dev-login in production environments. Refuses to trust forward headers (`X-Forwarded-For`, `X-Forwarded-Host`, `X-Real-IP`, `Forwarded`, `cf-connecting-ip`, `true-client-ip`), verifying direct client TCP peer socket (`client.host in ('127.0.0.1', '::1', 'testclient')`).

#### D. Database Dual-Binding & Persistence Layer (`core/db/`)
* **Local Fast-Path:** SQLite database [`reports.db`](file:///Users/lyndonpinto/Documents/Stock_Research_App/reports.db) serves high-throughput reads in single-digit milliseconds.
* **Cloud Persistence:** Every insert or update replicates asynchronously to Supabase cloud PostgreSQL.
* **Immutable Auditing:** Table `report_revisions` retains complete historical qualitative snapshots, timestamps, and LLM prompts.
* **PostgreSQL Dialect Parity & Standard SQL Upserts:** Enforces zero SQLite-only `INSERT OR REPLACE` or `INSERT OR IGNORE` statements across `core/db/`, `core/analysis/`, `core/msme/`, and `core/ingestion/`. All dual-bound database operations strictly utilize ANSI/PostgreSQL-compatible `ON CONFLICT (...) DO NOTHING` or `ON CONFLICT (...) DO UPDATE` syntax, ensuring 100% crash-free execution on Supabase Postgres.
* **Atomic Credit Deduction Race-Condition Shield ([`core/db/users.py:deduct_user_credits`](file:///Users/lyndonpinto/Documents/Stock_Research_App/core/db/users.py)):** Eradicates check-then-act concurrency race conditions by using a single atomic SQL update `UPDATE user_accounts SET credits_balance = ROUND(credits_balance - ?, 2), updated_at = ? WHERE id = ? AND credits_balance >= ? RETURNING credits_balance;`. If zero rows are returned/updated, the transaction rolls back immediately, preventing negative credit balances even under high concurrent multi-tab or multi-threaded load.
* **Database-Level Payment Idempotency Engine ([`core/db/connection.py`](file:///Users/lyndonpinto/Documents/Stock_Research_App/core/db/connection.py), [`core/db/users.py:add_user_credits`](file:///Users/lyndonpinto/Documents/Stock_Research_App/core/db/users.py)):** Enforces a strict disk-level `UNIQUE(gateway_payment_id)` constraint on `credit_transactions` (schema & migration `v035`), catching integrity violations and rolling back safely to prevent duplicate credit minting from replayed payment webhooks.
* **SEBI System Boundary Directive & Prescriptive Output Scrubber ([`core/agents/copilot/research_desk.py`](file:///Users/lyndonpinto/Documents/Stock_Research_App/core/agents/copilot/research_desk.py), [`core/agents/copilot/forensic_desk.py`](file:///Users/lyndonpinto/Documents/Stock_Research_App/core/agents/copilot/forensic_desk.py), [`web/routers/admin.py`](file:///Users/lyndonpinto/Documents/Stock_Research_App/web/routers/admin.py)):** Appends an immutable `SEBI_SYSTEM_BOUNDARY_DIRECTIVE` at the very end of research desk and copilot prompts after all user input to defeat prompt injection jailbreaks. Passes all generated and streaming text through `scrub_prescriptive_tokens()` and `scrub_streaming_yield()` with negative-lookahead regex to redact investment advisory tokens (`BUY`, `SELL`, `Target Price`, `Accumulate`, `Reduce`, `Stop Loss`) while preserving legitimate market terminology (`buyback`, `buy-side`, `buyers`, `sellers`).
* **Actor-Scoped AI QoS Governor, Denial-of-Wallet Shield & Single-Flight Coalescer ([`core/billing/qos_governor.py`](file:///Users/lyndonpinto/Documents/Stock_Research_App/core/billing/qos_governor.py), [`core/analytics/ai_qos_governor.py`](file:///Users/lyndonpinto/Documents/Stock_Research_App/core/analytics/ai_qos_governor.py)):** 3-lane priority QoS gate, velocity governor, and concurrency coalescer ensuring paying allocators and unlocked reports never experience degraded reports due to bot traffic or background cron spend. Lane 1 (ANON / Free-Tier) enforces zero paid LLM burn with a 30 req/min IP burst cap. Lane 2 (BACKGROUND_DAEMON) enforces an isolated $5.00/day daily spend ceiling (`MAX_DAEMON_DAILY_SPEND_USD`), failing over gracefully to deterministic institutional 7-pillar synthesis to preserve VIP quota. Lane 3 (USER_SYNTHESIS / USER_COPILOT) provides a VIP lane immune to daemon/bot activity, protected by per-user hourly velocity caps (40 copilot turns/hr, 20 syntheses/hr). Coalesces concurrent ticker syntheses with `single_flight_ticker_lock` to ensure only 1 live LLM execution per ticker while waiters reuse the freshly cached database report. Exported across `core.billing`, `core.analytics`, and `core.cortex`.<br>• **Denial-of-Wallet Shield (Cache-Miss Flooding Protection):** Protects the platform's Gemini API budget against adversarial bot scripts querying thousands of penny stocks to trigger >5% volatility cache-misses. Enforces strict daily live generation limits (`MAX_DAILY_LIVE_GENERATIONS_PER_USER = 25`, `MAX_DAILY_LIVE_GENERATIONS_PER_IP = 50`) tracked via thread-safe sliding windows (`_USER_DAILY_LIVE_GEN_WINDOWS`, `_IP_DAILY_LIVE_GEN_WINDOWS`) guarded by `threading.RLock`. Once breached, `synthesize_report` and `generate_stock_report` immediately fall back to serving cached historical report data without deducting user credits or invoking paid LLM tokens.<br>• **Strict Server-Side Paywall Redaction ([`core/agents/copilot/forensic_desk.py`](file:///Users/lyndonpinto/Documents/Stock_Research_App/core/agents/copilot/forensic_desk.py), [`web/common.py`](file:///Users/lyndonpinto/Documents/Stock_Research_App/web/common.py)):** Defeats Network Tab paywall bypasses where free users inspect JSON payloads. `redact_equity_intel_json()` and polymorphic `server_side_paywall_redact()` physically strip proprietary valuation and forensic models before HTTP dispatch: cleans `forensic_intelligence` (clearing checks, red flags, Beneish scores, setting `status="LOCKED"`) and `valuation_radar` (clearing `models`, `inputs`, `fair_value`, `margin_of_safety_pct`, setting `status="LOCKED"`), as well as proprietary `varan_delta_packet` and `setu_capital_matrix` structures whenever `_asset_unlocked_for_request(request, "EQUITY", canonical)` evaluates to False.
* **Quantitative Fixed-Income Math & Debt Dossier Invariants ([`core/analysis/debt_dossier.py`](file:///Users/lyndonpinto/Documents/Stock_Research_App/core/analysis/debt_dossier.py)):** Enforces rigorous mathematical invariants: Modified Duration = Macaulay Duration / (1 + y/k) < Macaulay Duration for positive yields ($y > 0$), and positive convexity ($C \ge 0$) in rate-shock scenarios ($\Delta P / P = -D_{\text{mod}} \cdot \Delta y + \frac{1}{2} C \cdot (\Delta y)^2$), dampening drawdowns during yield spikes and amplifying gains during rate cuts.
* **Section 115UA Tax Waterfall & REIT Dossier Invariants ([`core/analysis/reit_dossier.py`](file:///Users/lyndonpinto/Documents/Stock_Research_App/core/analysis/reit_dossier.py)):** Enforces statutory Section 115UA distribution splits (dividend, interest, return of capital/amortization, rental) summing to 100.0% ($\pm 0.01\%$) and mathematically proves Net Post-Tax Yield $\le$ Gross Pre-Tax Yield and Post-Tax Real Yield $\le$ Gross Pre-Tax Yield across all tax brackets and inflation rates.
* **Institutional Mutual Fund Look-Through & Active Share Invariants ([`core/analysis/fund_dossier.py`](file:///Users/lyndonpinto/Documents/Stock_Research_App/core/analysis/fund_dossier.py)):** Strictly withholds the Composite Health Score (`None`) whenever verified 7-pillar equity research coverage is $< 70.0\%$ (preventing unverified proxy extrapolation), and clamps True Active Share strictly to the valid mathematical interval $[0.0, 100.0]$.

Dual-bound on PostgreSQL and SQLite with `idx_remediation_status` and `idx_remediation_hash`.

Any unresolved finding aging 7+ days automatically caps overall platform health status at `ACTION_REQUIRED`.
  * **Closed-Loop 3-Step Execution Cycle:**
    * **Step 1 (Verify First):** Re-tests all currently `OPEN` action points against their specific invariants via targeted O(1) dispatched `verify_invariant(finding)`. Passing tests immediately transition findings to `RESOLVED`, recover score deductions, and archive items from active queue. Failing tests increment consecutive failure counters and verification attempts.
    * **Step 2 (Explore Next):** Executes broad invariant scans across Backend Stability (`INV_UNBOUNDED_CACHE`, `INV_SILENT_EXCEPTION_SWALLOW`), Dual-Engine Database Parity (`INV_SQL_DIALECT_COMPAT`, `INV_DB_SCHEMA_BOUNDS`), UI/UX Contracts (`INV_DOM_INTEGRITY`, `INV_UI_NO_DEAD_HOVER`), Security & Vulnerabilities (`INV_SEC_HEADERS`, `INV_SEC_AUTH_SCOPE`, `INV_SEC_NO_STACKTRACE`), and Strategy (`INV_SEBI_SAFE_HARBOR`). Inserts or updates findings via single-transaction batch commit (`record_findings_batch`).
    * **Step 3 (Publish Score & Report):** Computes composite health score factoring progressive aging deductions and generates prioritized deliverables.

#### G. Executive Governance & Operations Copilot Layer (`core/agents/copilot/admin_copilot.py`)
* **Admin Executive Copilot Coordinator ([`core/agents/copilot/admin_copilot.py`](file:///Users/lyndonpinto/Documents/Stock_Research_App/core/agents/copilot/admin_copilot.py)):**
  Autonomous operational governance assistant serving verified platform administrators. Answers natural language queries regarding live site telemetry, visitor paths, cash flow, support grievances, and system audits.
* **Grounded Analytical Ingestion Tools:**
  * `tool_get_admin_dashboard_kpis(window, exclude_tests)`: Retrieves live executive counts across distinct visitor footprints, total user actions, cache efficiency %, estimated API cost saved ($USD heuristic), and honest cash receipts.
  * `tool_get_billables_summary(window, limit, exclude_tests)`: Retrieves recent billing ledger transactions and net cash receipts (Gross less refunds).
  * `tool_get_support_tickets_summary(status, limit, exclude_tests)`: Ingests pending customer grievances and complaint dockets.
  * `tool_lookup_user_account(identifier)`: Queries user profiles, active subscription tiers, credit balances, and usage history.
  * `tool_get_admin_audit_trail(limit)`: Ingests immutable administrative actions from `admin_audit_log`.
* **Two-Step Confirmation Architecture (`create_action_proposal`):**
  Strictly prohibits autonomous execution of financial or destructive state mutations. When an administrator requests an operational change (credit allocation, ticket resolution, refund, test data purge, or asset scan), the copilot formulates an interactive action proposal card with `requires_confirmation: True`, payload details, and impact warnings.
* **Self-Directed Credit Allocation & Auto-Provisioning (`core/agents/copilot/admin_copilot.py`, `core/db/admin.py`):**
  Recognizes natural self-targeted credit requests ("send some credits to myself", "give me 10 credits", "add credits to my account") using strict word-boundary matching `\b(myself|me|my\s+account|my\s+email|self|mine|to\s+me|for\s+me|to\s+myself)\b` across GenAI prompts and deterministic fallbacks, resolving the recipient target cleanly to the authenticated administrator's email (`admin_user.get('email')`). If the recipient account does not exist in `user_accounts` (e.g., an administrator logged in via `admin_users` without a consumer record), `admin_grant_user_credits` automatically provisions the account via `get_or_create_user(..., auto_provision=True)` rather than failing with an unprovisioned user error.
* **Role-Based Access Control & Audit Trail (`execute_confirmed_admin_action`):**
  Validates administrator role against `ALLOWED_ROLES_PER_ACTION` (`owner`, `admin`, `super_admin`, `superadmin`, `viewer`). Automatically normalizes bootstrap owner emails (`get_authorized_bootstrap_emails()`) to supreme `owner` privileges and `super_admin`/`superadmin` to `admin`. Rejects mutating actions from viewers with 403 Forbidden. Commits executed actions through verified DB routines and immutably logs every mutation to `admin_audit_log`.
* **Model Cascade & Resilient Fallback:**
  Invokes Google GenAI cascade (`gemini-3.8-flash`, `gemini-3.5-flash-lite`, `gemini-3.1-flash-lite`) with 10s timeout. Falls back seamlessly to deterministic regex intent parsing (`parse_deterministic_admin_query`) when offline or in test environments. Active frontend proposals are stored in `window.activeCopilotProposals`, eliminating HTML JSON quote escaping syntax errors.

#### H. Platform Paywall, Credit Entitlement & Compute Cost Policy

The platform operates under a strict, economically grounded **Compute & Data Ingestion Cost Attribution Model**:
* **The Core Practice / Architectural Principle:** Any section, tool, or analytical pipeline that consumes ongoing, variable external server resources—such as real-time market depth APIs (NSE/BSE/SmartAPI), continuous web scraping, AMFI portfolio constituent crawling, or generative AI LLM token inference (`gemini-3.8-flash`)—or that provides high-value downloadable institutional artifacts (unwatermarked PDF research dossiers, full forensic accounting ledgers, reverse DCF solvers, pre-mortem failure mode ledgers) **MUST require computational research credits (1 credit per single pass, or an active Pro membership)**.
* **The Open-Access (Paywall-Free) Principle:** Any surface, reference directory, benchmark matrix, pre-generated baseline overview, or client-side financial calculator that does **not** consume incremental external data or AI inference once seeded/generated **MUST remain 100% free and open without a paywall**.

#### I. Observability & Telemetry Architecture: Better Stack Integration (`core/logging_config.py`)

* **Centralized Standard Library Logging Integration (`core/logging_config.py`):**
  Seamlessly connects Python standard library `logging` to Better Stack (Logtail) via `logtail.LogtailHandler`:
  * **Target Ingestion Host:** Configured to `s2808842.us-west-2a.betterstackdata.com` (or configurable via `BETTER_STACK_INGESTING_HOST` / `LOGTAIL_INGESTING_HOST`).
  * **Source Token:** Secured via `n9BuFhtn6SwGdLrRd4A8wFn6` (or configurable via `BETTER_STACK_SOURCE_TOKEN` / `LOGTAIL_SOURCE_TOKEN`).
  * **Hierarchy Attachment:** Attached to the `equity_research` namespace and root logger, routing all web routes, APIs, schedulers, background workers, and cortex engines automatically to Better Stack.
  * **Structured Logging Support:** Supports structured contextual attributes via standard `logger.info("message", extra={"key": "value"})` per the official Better Stack integration guide.
  * **FastAPI Lifespan & Shutdown Hook (`web/main.py`):** Initialized at application boot; queues are flushed synchronously during lifespan termination (`flush_betterstack_logs`) to prevent in-flight log drops on container restarts.
  * **Automated Test Isolation Guard:** Detects automated test runners (`is_test_environment()`), isolating test runs to local console output without consuming Better Stack quotas or failing sandboxed test suites.
  * **CLI Verification Tool (`scripts/test_betterstack.py`):** Diagnostic verification probe transmitting structured logs directly to Better Stack live tail (`https://telemetry.betterstack.com/team/0/tail`).

## 3. Operational Process Flows for Audit Verification

Auditors evaluating whether production on `main` complies with standards can trace these 6 deterministic workflows:

### Process Flow 1: Morning 9:00 AM Discovery & Ingestion Pipeline
```mermaid
sequenceDiagram
    autonumber
    participant Cron as 9:00 AM IST Cron / Scheduler
    participant Disc as core/analysis/stock_discovery.py
    participant BSE as BSE / Angel One Feeds
    participant Chan as ChanakyaGate (core/cortex/chanakya.py)
    participant DB as SQLite reports.db & Supabase

    Cron->>Disc: Trigger refresh_daily_discovery()
    Disc->>BSE: Pull active NSE/BSE universe quotes & filings
    Disc->>Chan: Run 10-Point Clean-Room Sieve (mode="SOFT")
    Chan-->>Disc: Clean-Room Score & Forensic Flags
    Disc->>Disc: Rank Top 10 Growth / Safety Opportunities
    Disc->>DB: Persist discovery cohort & mark 'today' edition
    Disc-->>Cron: Pipeline Complete (200 OK)
```

### Process Flow 2: Live Equity Dossier Request Flow (`GET /dossier/{ticker}`)
```mermaid
sequenceDiagram
    autonumber
    participant User as Client Browser
    participant Router as web/main.py
    participant Cache as SQLite reports.db
    participant Intel as compile_equity_dossier()
    participant Angel as AngelOneGateway (AWS Proxy)
    participant Cortex as ValuationRadar, SectorScoring, ForensicSieve
    participant UI as Jinja2 Template (dossier.html)

    User->>Router: GET /dossier/{ticker}
    Router->>Cache: Query cached 7-pillar report & metadata
    Cache-->>Router: Stored Qualitative Dossier (Gemini search-grounded)
    Router->>Intel: compile_equity_dossier(ticker, fund_data)
    Intel->>Angel: Fetch Live Quote, LTP & Level-2 Depth
    Angel-->>Intel: Live LTP, Imbalance Ratio, Spread bps
    Intel->>Cortex: Compute Valuation Radar, Sector Score & Forensic Sieve
    Cortex-->>Intel: Fair Value, Margin of Safety, Forensic Flags
    Intel-->>Router: Composite Cockpit Payload & Score
    Router->>UI: Hydrate dossier.html with Cockpit metrics & 7 Pillars
    UI-->>User: Rendered Institutional Dossier (< 50ms)
```

### Process Flow 3: Event-Driven Announcement Classification Flow (Garuda API)
```mermaid
sequenceDiagram
    autonumber
    participant Client as Client / Admin Console
    participant Router as POST /api/cortex/garuda/classify
    participant Garuda as GarudaReflexEngine (garuda.py)

    Client->>Router: POST /api/cortex/garuda/classify (Symbol, Headline, Details)
    Router->>Garuda: classify_announcement(symbol, headline, details)
    Garuda->>Garuda: Regex taxonomy matching against 7 statutory pillars
    Garuda->>Garuda: Generate MicroSnapshotDelta (Target Pillar, Impact Severity, Summary)
    Garuda-->>Router: Structured MicroSnapshotDelta
    Router-->>Client: 200 OK (Surgical Delta Classification Payload)
```

### Process Flow 4: Pre-Commit & Checkpoint Verification Gate
```mermaid
flowchart TD
    Start([Developer / Agent Prepares Commit]) --> Step1[Run Unit Tests: python3 -m unittest discover -s tests]
    Step1 --> TestDecision{All Tests Pass?}
    TestDecision -- No --> FixErrors[Debug & Fix Engine Vulnerabilities] --> Step1
    TestDecision -- Yes --> Step2[Execute git commit]
    Step2 --> Step3[Run ./run.sh backup - Single Post-Commit Backup]
    Step3 --> Step4[Run ./run.sh checkpoint]
    Step4 --> Step5[Freeze SQLite DB to .checkpoints/]
    Step6 --> Step7[Create Annotated Git Tag]
    Step7 --> Step8[Sync dev branch with main]
    Step8 --> Step9[Push git push origin main && git push origin --tags]
    Step9 --> Done([Production Main Certified & Synced])
```

Admin Console features `#adminTicketReplyModal` with live ticket metadata, message history thread, quick template chips (`📋 Investigating`, `✅ Resolved`, `🎁 +2 Credits`, `❓ Clarification`), status switcher, email trigger toggle, and remedial credit allocation.<br>• Asynchronous SPA Ticket Reply Flow (`POST /api/admin/tickets/reply` & `handleTicketReplySubmit`): Upgraded `#ticketReplyForm` to submit asynchronously via `fetch('/api/admin/tickets/reply')`. Displays tactile progress state (`⏳ Dispatching Response...`), resets the form on HTTP 200, triggers `window.showToast()`, and automatically re-fetches the conversation thread via `await openTicketReplyModal(res.ticket_id)` without reloading the page or losing admin filter state.<br>• Anti-Bot Honeypot, Disposable Email & Scanner Payload Defense (`web/main.py`, `web/templates/contact.html`): Form embeds accessible off-screen input `#contactWebsiteUrl` (`name="website_url"`, `tabindex="-1"`, `autocomplete="off"`). Handler enforces triple-defense filtering rejecting: (1) populated honeypots, (2) disposable/temporary domains (`_DISPOSABLE_EMAIL_DOMAINS` including `nolettersbox.com`, `mailinator.com`, `guerrillamail.com`, `tempmail.com`), and (3) automated scanner gibberish payloads (`_is_bot_gibberish_ticket`) detecting unbroken 16+ alphanumeric tokens or shared 5+ digit correlation identifiers across name, subject, and message. Returns HTTP 400 with actionable feedback without polluting tickets or dispatching alerts.<br>• Automated Bot & Spam Ticket Purge Routine (`core/db/telemetry.py:purge_test_telemetry`): Maintenance purge routine purges test and spam support tickets matching test domains, `@nolettersbox.com`, `@domain.org`, or unbroken 16+ character messages (`LENGTH(TRIM(message)) >= 16 AND TRIM(message) NOT LIKE '% %'`), while strictly preserving legitimate investor support tickets. If credentials are unconfigured in `.env`/environment, `send_ticket_reply_email` returns `False` and logs to console, preventing false positive `✓ Email Delivered` indicators in the database and UI. ## 5. Maintenance Protocol: Mandatory Living Documentation Synchronization

Per Directive 3 in [`AGENTS.md`](file:///Users/lyndonpinto/Documents/Stock_Research_App/AGENTS.md), **documentation must be updated synchronously in the exact same commit whenever code is modified**. To prevent documentation decay, follow this non-negotiable Standard Operating Procedure:

### Standard Operating Procedure (SOP)
1. **Whenever a New Engine, Route, or Gateway is Created/Modified:**
   - Update **Section 1 (Site Inventory & Route Breakdown)** with new endpoints, modal triggers, and UI templates.
   - Document its mathematical formulation and layer in **Section 2 (Architecture & Component Breakdown)**.
   - Update Mermaid diagrams in **Section 3 (Operational Process Flows)** to reflect real runtime handshakes.
   - Add/update a row in **Section 4 (Code-to-Feature Navigation Matrix)** with primary functions, callers, and diagnostic failure fixes.
2. **Whenever Report Generation, Ingestion, or Criteria Change:**
   - Update [`docs/REPORT_EVALUATION_FRAMEWORKS.md`](file:///Users/lyndonpinto/Documents/Stock_Research_App/docs/REPORT_EVALUATION_FRAMEWORKS.md) and [`.antigravity/docs/EVALUATION_FRAMEWORKS.md`](file:///Users/lyndonpinto/Documents/Stock_Research_App/.antigravity/docs/EVALUATION_FRAMEWORKS.md) with updated mathematical models, TTLs, and data gateways.
3. **Whenever a Bug or Adversarial Edge-Case is Hardened:**
   - Record the discovery and remedy in Section 4.
   - Add a corresponding test case to [`tests/audit/test_adversarial_stress.py`](file:///Users/lyndonpinto/Documents/Stock_Research_App/tests/audit/test_adversarial_stress.py).
4. **Whenever Creating a Checkpoint (`./run.sh checkpoint`):**
   - Update **Section 6 (Historical Build Ledger)** with the new tag, commit hash, timestamp, and backup folder.
   - Sync this manual to `main`, `dev`, and remote `origin`.
5. **Before Stating Task Completion:**
   - Adhere to Directive 5 in [`AGENTS.md`](file:///Users/lyndonpinto/Documents/Stock_Research_App/AGENTS.md): Empirically double-check that every code change, test assertion, and documentation update discussed in the session is physically present on disk and active in runtime before marking any task as complete.

### End of Engineering Manual

```bash
./run.sh rollback <CHECKPOINT_TAG>
```
*To execute the full automated verification audit, run:*
```bash
./run.sh check
python3 -m unittest discover -s tests
```
